MAP3K7

UniProt ID: O43318
Organism: Homo sapiens
Review Status: COMPLETE
📝 Provide Detailed Feedback
224 Annotations 3 Core Functions 120 References 1 Documents 3 Questions 2 Experiments Raw YAML

Gene Description

MAP3K7 encodes TAK1 (TGF-beta-activated kinase 1), a TKL-group serine/threonine protein kinase that acts as a mitogen-activated protein kinase kinase kinase (MAP3K). TAK1 has an N-terminal kinase domain and a C-terminal region that binds the adaptors TAB2 and TAB3; the activator TAB1 binds near the kinase domain. TAK1-TAB1-TAB2/TAB3 complexes are recruited to and activated by K63-linked and unanchored polyubiquitin chains, which are bound by the TAB2/TAB3 zinc fingers, leading to autophosphorylation of the TAK1 activation loop (Thr184/Thr187/Ser192). Activated TAK1 phosphorylates MKK3/MKK6 and MKK4/MKK7, driving p38 and JNK MAP kinase cascades, and phosphorylates IKKbeta to activate canonical NF-kappaB signaling. TAK1 is the shared kinase relay downstream of TRAF6 and related ubiquitin ligases in IL-1 receptor, Toll-like receptor, TNF receptor, NOD-like receptor, antigen receptor and TGF-beta/BMP signaling, and it is inactivated by PP6/PP2A-type phosphatases. Other reported substrates include AMPK and STING. TAK1 acts mainly in the cytosol after recruitment to receptor-proximal complexes. Heterozygous MAP3K7 variants cause frontometaphyseal dysplasia 2 and cardiospondylocarpofacial syndrome.

GO:0000165 MAPK cascade
IDA
PMID:11865055
IAP suppression of apoptosis involves distinct mechanisms: t... 		ACCEPT
Summary: TAK1 is the MAP3K tier of JNK and p38 MAPK cascades.
Reason: TAK1 phosphorylates and activates MAP2Ks (MKK3/MKK6, MKK4/MKK7) that in turn activate p38 and JNK. Participation in the MAPK cascade is a direct, core role of the kinase.
Supporting Evidence:
PMID:11865055
The MAP3 kinase, TAK1, appears to be an essential component of this antiapoptotic pathway since IAP-mediated activation of JNK1, as well as protection against TNF-alpha- and ICE-induced apoptosis, is inhibited when catalytically inactive TAK1 is expressed.
PMID:9079627
Expression of a constitutively active form of TAK1 resulted in activation of SAPK/JNK and SEK1/MKK4, a direct activator of SAPK/JNK.

GO:0001223 transcription coactivator binding
IEA
GO_REF:0000107 		MARK AS OVER ANNOTATED
Summary: Transcription coactivator binding transferred from rat; no human evidence.
Reason: Electronic ortholog transfer from rat. TAK1 is a cytoplasmic kinase and binding to transcription coactivators is not part of its established mechanism; any such interactions are context-specific and do not define its function.
GO:0001649 osteoblast differentiation
IEA
GO_REF:0000107 		KEEP AS NON CORE
Summary: Osteoblast differentiation transferred from mouse; downstream developmental context.
Reason: TAK1 signaling downstream of TGF-beta/BMP influences skeletal cell differentiation, and MAP3K7 gain-of-function causes frontometaphyseal dysplasia in humans, but TAK1 acts via its general kinase relay rather than as a dedicated osteoblast-differentiation factor. Retain as non-core.
Supporting Evidence:
PMID:27426733
The recurrent mutation does not destabilize TAK1, or impair its ability to homodimerize or bind TAB2, but it does increase TAK1 autophosphorylation
GO:0001666 response to hypoxia
IEA
GO_REF:0000107 		KEEP AS NON CORE
Summary: TAK1 is activated by hypoxia and relays it to JNK via JIP1.
Reason: Ortholog-based annotation supported in human cells: hypoxia increases TAK1 activity and TAK1-JIP1-JNK signaling shapes the hypoxic stress response. This is one of many stress inputs; non-core.
Supporting Evidence:
PMID:17709393
We report that TAK1 is able to form a stable complex with JIP1 and thus regulate the activation of JNK, which in turn determines the cellular stress response to hypoxia.

GO:0002755 MyD88-dependent toll-like receptor signaling pathway
TAS
Reactome:R-HSA-166058 		ACCEPT
Summary: TAK1 is the kinase relay of MyD88-IRAK-TRAF6 signaling from TLRs/IL-1R.
Reason: MyD88-dependent TLR signaling converges on TRAF6-generated K63 chains that activate the TAK1-TAB complex, which phosphorylates IKKbeta and MAP2Ks. This is one of the canonical inputs that define TAK1 function.

GO:0004674 protein serine/threonine kinase activity
EXP
PMID:10702308
TAK1 mitogen-activated protein kinase kinase kinase is activ... 		ACCEPT
Summary: TAK1 has Ser/Thr protein kinase activity, shown directly in vitro.
Reason: TAK1 autophosphorylates its activation loop (Ser192, Thr184/187) and phosphorylates Ser/Thr residues on MAP2Ks, IKKbeta and other substrates (AMPK, STING Ser355). Core activity, family-wide in PTHR46716.
Supporting Evidence:
PMID:10702308
These results suggest that IL-1 and ectopic expression of TAB1 both activate TAK1 via autophosphorylation of Ser-192.

GO:0004707 MAP kinase activity
IDA
PMID:11865055
IAP suppression of apoptosis involves distinct mechanisms: t... 		MODIFY
Summary: TAK1 is a MAP3K, not a MAPK; the assay in this paper measured JNK1 activity downstream of TAK1.
Reason: Full text of PMID:11865055 shows that kinase assays used ATF-2 or MBP as substrates of immunoprecipitated JNK1; TAK1 was manipulated with a catalytically inactive mutant and acts upstream of JNK1. TAK1 does not phosphorylate transcription-factor substrates as a MAP kinase. The essence (TAK1 kinase activity in the JNK pathway) is better captured by MAP kinase kinase kinase activity.
Proposed replacements: MAP kinase kinase kinase activity
Supporting Evidence:
PMID:11865055
After immunoprecipitation, an in vitro kinase assay was performed using ATF-2 or MBP as substrates.
PMID:11865055
The MAP3 kinase, TAK1, appears to be an essential component of this antiapoptotic pathway since IAP-mediated activation of JNK1, as well as protection against TNF-alpha- and ICE-induced apoptosis, is inhibited when catalytically inactive TAK1 is expressed.
GO:0004708 MAP kinase kinase activity
IEA
GO_REF:0000107 		MARK AS OVER ANNOTATED
Summary: MAP kinase kinase activity transferred from mouse; TAK1 acts one tier higher.
Reason: TAK1 phosphorylates MAP2Ks (MKK3/4/6/7), i.e. it is a MAP3K. Activation of the atypical MAPK NLK by TAK1 has been reported, but direct MAP2K-type phosphorylation of a MAPK is not the established activity of human TAK1.
Supporting Evidence:
PMID:8663074
MKK3 was also shown to be a good substrate for TAK1 in vitro.
PMID:15764709
Here, we show that TGF-beta-activated kinase 1 (TAK1) interacts with STAT3, that the TAK1-Nemo-like kinase (NLK) pathway is efficiently activated by IL-6 through the YXXQ motif
GO:0004709 MAP kinase kinase kinase activity
EXP
PMID:11460167
TAK1 is a ubiquitin-dependent kinase of MKK and IKK. 		ACCEPT

PMID:11460167
the activity of TAK1 to phosphorylate MKK6, which activates the JNK-p38 kinase pathway, is directly regulated by K63-linked polyubiquitination

Summary: Interaction with the E3 ubiquitin ligase TRAF6.

Summary: MAP4K activity reflects TAK1 autophosphorylation events, not phosphorylation of a separate MAP3K.
Reason: The Reactome reactions cited are "Auto phosphorylation of TAK1" / "Activation of recruited TAK1"; mapping a MAP3K autophosphorylating itself to MAP kinase kinase kinase kinase activity misrepresents its activity. The underlying Ser/Thr autophosphorylation is captured by protein serine/threonine kinase activity. The early report of TAK1 phosphorylating the MAP3K NIK (PMID:10094049) is not the basis of the modern model, where TAK1 phosphorylates IKKbeta directly.
Proposed replacements: protein serine/threonine kinase activity
Supporting Evidence:
PMID:10702308
These results suggest that IL-1 and ectopic expression of TAB1 both activate TAK1 via autophosphorylation of Ser-192.
PMID:10838074
Autophosphorylation of two threonine residues in the activation loop of TAK1 was necessary for TAK1 activation.

The kinase TAK1 can activate the NIK-I kappaB as well as the... 		MARK AS OVER ANNOTATED
Summary: Based on the 1999 TAK1-NIK model.
Reason: PMID:10094049 proposed TAK1 phosphorylates NIK to activate IKKalpha in IL-1 signaling. The subsequent model (TAK1 complex directly phosphorylates IKKbeta) superseded this, and NIK-driven non-canonical NF-kappaB is generally TAK1-independent. Retaining TAK1 in non-canonical NF-kappaB as a direct positive regulator overstates the evidence; the abstract-only cache prevents full adjudication, so this is not removed.

IDA
PMID:11460167
TAK1 is a ubiquitin-dependent kinase of MKK and IKK. 		NEW
Summary: TAK1 is the catalytic subunit of the TAK1-TAB1-TAB2/TAB3 complex (TRIKA2).
Reason: The purified IKK-activating factor TRIKA2 is composed of TAK1, TAB1 and TAB2; TAB3 substitutes for TAB2. GO:0097076 is defined as exactly this complex (catalytic TAK1 plus TAB regulatory subunits) but currently carries no annotations in GOA; ComplexPortal annotates TAK1 to the generic GO:1902554. This is a component membership, not a pathway-participation claim, so the NEW process bar does not apply; the PTHR46716 family review scopes GO:0097076 to SF1.
Supporting Evidence:
PMID:11460167

PMID:8638164
TAB1 and TAK1 were co-immunoprecipitated from mammalian cells.
PMID:14633987
These results suggest that TAB2 and TAB3 function redundantly as mediators of TAK1 activation in IL-1 and TNF signal transduction.

Core Functions

MAP kinase kinase kinase of the stress/inflammatory MAPK pathways: ubiquitin-activated TAK1 phosphorylates and activates MKK3/MKK6 (p38) and MKK4/MKK7 (JNK).

Molecular Function:
MAP kinase kinase kinase activity
Directly Involved In:
JNK cascade p38MAPK cascade
Cellular Locations:
cytosol
In Complex:
transforming growth factor beta activated kinase 1 complex
Supporting Evidence:
  • PMID:8663074
    MKK3 was also shown to be a good substrate for TAK1 in vitro.
  • PMID:9079627
    Expression of a constitutively active form of TAK1 resulted in activation of SAPK/JNK and SEK1/MKK4, a direct activator of SAPK/JNK.
  • PMID:11460167
    the activity of TAK1 to phosphorylate MKK6, which activates the JNK-p38 kinase pathway, is directly regulated by K63-linked polyubiquitination

IKKbeta-activating kinase of canonical NF-kappaB signaling: the TAK1-TAB complex, activated by TRAF6-generated K63-linked and unanchored polyubiquitin bound by TAB2/TAB3, phosphorylates IKKbeta downstream of IL-1R/TLR, TNF and other receptors.

Molecular Function:
protein serine/threonine kinase activity
Directly Involved In:
canonical NF-kappaB signal transduction interleukin-1-mediated signaling pathway
Cellular Locations:
cytosol
In Complex:
transforming growth factor beta activated kinase 1 complex
Supporting Evidence:
  • PMID:11460167
    We find that the TAK1 kinase complex phosphorylates and activates IKK in a manner that depends on TRAF6 and Ubc13-Uev1A.
  • PMID:19675569
    free Lys 63 polyubiquitin chains, which are not conjugated to any target protein, directly activate TAK1 by binding to the ubiquitin receptor TAB2
  • PMID:14633987
    These results suggest that TAB2 and TAB3 function redundantly as mediators of TAK1 activation in IL-1 and TNF signal transduction.
  • PMID:10094049
    Here we show that the MAPKK kinase TAK1 acts upstream of NIK in the IL-1-activated signalling pathway and that TAK1 associates with TRAF6 during IL-1 signalling.

TGF-beta/BMP-activated MAP3K in non-Smad TGF-beta signaling: TbetaRI-associated TRAF6 K63-ubiquitinates and activates TAK1, which drives p38/JNK activation.

Molecular Function:
MAP kinase kinase kinase activity
Directly Involved In:
transforming growth factor beta receptor signaling pathway
Cellular Locations:
cytosol
Supporting Evidence:
  • PMID:8533096
    kinase activity of TAK1 was stimulated in response to TGF-beta and bone morphogenetic protein
  • PMID:18758450
    Intriguingly, TGF-beta-induced TRAF6-mediated Lys 63-linked polyubiquitylation of TAK1 Lys 34 correlates with TAK1 activation.
  • PMID:12589052
    Ectopic expression of wild-type TAK1 promoted TGF-beta1-induced phosphorylation of p38 and apoptosis, whereas dominant negative TAK1 reduced TGF-beta1-induced phosphorylation of p38 and apoptosis.

Suggested Questions for Experts

Q: GO:0097076 (TGF-beta activated kinase 1 complex) has no annotations in GOA, and ComplexPortal annotates TAK1-TAB complexes to the generic GO:1902554 serine/threonine protein kinase complex. Should GO:0097076 be made is_a GO:1902554 (it is currently only under GO:1902911 protein kinase complex) and used for TAK1, TAB1, TAB2 and TAB3?

Q: Is the 1999 TAK1-NIK-IKKalpha model (basis of the non-canonical NF-kappaB IMP annotation) still supported, or should TAK1 annotations to non-canonical NF-kappaB be retired in favour of direct IKKbeta activation?

Q: Which substrate-specific outputs of TAK1 (MKK3/6, MKK4/7, IKKbeta, AMPK, STING) are selected by TAB1 versus TAB2/TAB3-containing complexes in a given receptor context?

Suggested Experiments

Experiment: Reconstitute TAK1-TAB1, TAK1-TAB2 and TAK1-TAB3 complexes with defined K63, M1 and hybrid unanchored chains and measure phosphorylation kinetics on MKK4, MKK6, MKK7 and IKKbeta.

Hypothesis: Ubiquitin chain architecture and TAB composition tune TAK1 substrate selection between MAPK and IKK branches.

Experiment: Compare kinase activity, autophosphorylation and substrate phosphorylation of FMD-associated MAP3K7 variants (P485L, E70Q, V100E, G168R) with wild type in patient-derived fibroblasts.

Hypothesis: The recurrent P485L variant enhances TAB-dependent autoactivation without altering TAB2 binding, biasing downstream signaling.

📚 Additional Documentation

Notes

(MAP3K7-notes.md)

MAP3K7 (TAK1) review notes

Provenance / process

  • 2026-10-05: de-novo review. GOA (223 rows), UniProt and stub YAML fetched earlier; publications cached with
    just fetch-gene-pmids human MAP3K7 (57/57). Extra PMIDs cached with just fetch-pmid:
    8533096 (TAK1 discovery), 14633987 (TAB3), 15327770 (TAB2/TAB3 K63-Ub binding).
  • Deep research: the falcon provider is known to fail for this batch (HTTP 402), so it was not attempted.
    No -deep-research-*.md file exists for this gene; these notes are built from cached primary literature,
    the UniProt record and the PTHR46716 family review.
  • Most key TAK1 papers are cached abstract-only (full_text_available: false): 8663074, 9079627, 10094049,
    10702308, 10838074, 10882101, 11460167, 8638164. Full text available for 11865055, 12242293, 12589052,
    14982987, 17079228, 17158449, 19675569, 20538596, 21512573, 25371197, 27426733 and several proteomics screens.

Identity and architecture

  • MAP3K7 / TAK1, 606 aa (isoform 1B is canonical; 4 splice isoforms), TKL-group Ser/Thr kinase; N-terminal
    kinase domain (catalytic K63, HRD D156), C-terminal TAB2/TAB3-binding region (residues ~479-553)
    PMID:17158449.
  • Discovered in a yeast MAPK-pathway complementation screen as a MAPKKK stimulated by TGF-beta and BMP
    PMID:8533096.

Molecular function: MAP3K / Ser-Thr kinase

  • Phosphorylates and activates MKK6 and MKK3 (p38 branch)
    PMID:8663074.
  • Activates MKK4/SEK1 -> JNK; activated by ceramide
    PMID:9079627.
  • TAK1 complex (TRIKA2 = TAK1+TAB1+TAB2) phosphorylates IKK (IKKbeta) and MKK6 in a K63-ubiquitin-dependent way
    PMID:11460167.
  • Activation is by autophosphorylation in the activation loop (Ser192; Thr184/Thr187)
    PMID:10702308;
    PMID:10838074.
  • Inactivated by PP6 dephosphorylation of Thr187 PMID:17079228.
  • Other direct substrates: AMPK (in vitro) PMID:20538596, STING Ser355 PMID:37832545,
    Ror2 C-terminus PMID:18762249. The 1999 claim that TAK1 phosphorylates NIK PMID:10094049 predates the
    TAK1->IKKbeta model; NIK is now understood as the non-canonical NF-kB kinase acting independently of TAK1.

Complex: TAK1-TAB1-TAB2/TAB3

  • TAB1 is a constitutive activator PMID:8638164.
  • TAB2 links TAK1 to TRAF6 in IL-1 signaling PMID:10882101.
  • TAB3 is redundant with TAB2 in IL-1 and TNF signaling PMID:14633987.
  • TAB2/TAB3 NZF domains are K63-polyubiquitin receptors; TAB2 binds ubiquitinated RIP after TNF
    PMID:15327770.
  • Unanchored K63 chains directly activate TAK1 via TAB2 PMID:19675569.
  • GO term GO:0097076 (TGF-beta activated kinase 1 complex) exists, is unrestricted, but has zero annotations in GOA
    (QuickGO, 2026-10-05). ComplexPortal annotates MAP3K7 to the generic GO:1902554 serine/threonine protein kinase
    complex (IPI, PMID:22158122). GO:0097076 is_a GO:1902911 protein kinase complex, but NOT is_a GO:1902554
    (ontology placement gap). Proposed as NEW (part_of GO:0097076) in this review.

Pathways (upstream inputs)

  • IL-1R/TLR (MyD88-IRAK-TRAF6) [PMID:10094049; PMID:12242293 membrane complex II then cytosolic activation].
  • TLR3/TRIF -> TRAF6-TAK1-TAB2 -> NF-kB, not IRF3 PMID:14982987.
  • TLR4 (ECSIT complex) PMID:25371197. TNF via TAB2/TAB3 binding to ubiquitinated RIP1 [PMID:15327770, PMID:14633987].
  • TGF-beta: TbetaRI-TRAF6 activates TAK1 (K63-Ub of TAK1 Lys34) -> p38/JNK PMID:18758450; TGF-beta-induced apoptosis via TAK1-MKK3-p38 PMID:12589052.
  • TCR: BCL10-MALT1-TRAF6-TAK1 -> IKK, IL-2 PMID:15125833.
  • NOD2 PMID:15075345, TRIM5 capsid sensing via free K63 chains PMID:21512573, STING trafficking PMID:37832545.

Disease

  • Frontometaphyseal dysplasia 2 / cardiospondylocarpofacial syndrome: MAP3K7 gain-of-function (P485L)
    PMID:27426733.

Curation decisions summary

  • Core MF: GO:0004709 MAP3K activity and GO:0004674 Ser/Thr kinase (IKKbeta, MKK3/4/6/7). GO:0106310 rows accepted.
  • GO:0004707 MAP kinase activity (IDA, PMID:11865055): full text shows JNK1 activity was assayed, not TAK1 as a MAPK ->
    MODIFY to GO:0004709.
  • GO:0008349 MAP4K activity (Reactome TAS, mouse IEA): Reactome events are TAK1 autophosphorylation; this is not
    MAP4K activity on a distinct MAP3K -> MODIFY to GO:0004674 (autophosphorylation captured by the Ser/Thr kinase term).
  • 54 protein-binding IPI rows: REMOVE (uninformative) except MODIFY where a specific partner class is clear
    (E3 ligases -> GO:0031625; PPP6C -> GO:0019903; JIP1 -> GO:0097110; TGFBR1 -> GO:0034713).
  • Downstream physiology (osteoblast, bone development, hypoxia, angiotensin, vascular SMC, cell size, cell cycle, ROS)
    from rat/mouse orthology IEA: MARK_AS_OVER_ANNOTATED or KEEP_AS_NON_CORE.
  • histone kinase activity / chromatin remodeling IEA (rat): no human evidence; MARK_AS_OVER_ANNOTATED.

Module consistency (tnf_signaling, nlr_signaling)

  • Neither modules/tnf_signaling.yaml nor modules/nlr_signaling.yaml has a MAP3K7/TAK1 part, although both modules'
    falcon deep research names TAK1-TAB2/3 as the kinase relaying K63/M1-ubiquitin scaffolds to IKKbeta and MAPKs.
    The tnf "NF-kappaB and inflammatory output" step is carried by a "TRAF2 signal relay" part, and nlr by
    "RIPK2 inflammatory signal relay"; neither is the kinase that phosphorylates IKKbeta. Discrepancy reported to caller.

id: O43318
gene_symbol: MAP3K7
product_type: PROTEIN
status: COMPLETE
taxon:
  id: NCBITaxon:9606
  label: Homo sapiens
description: MAP3K7 encodes TAK1 (TGF-beta-activated kinase 1), a TKL-group serine/threonine protein kinase that
  acts as a mitogen-activated protein kinase kinase kinase (MAP3K). TAK1 has an N-terminal kinase domain and a C-terminal
  region that binds the adaptors TAB2 and TAB3; the activator TAB1 binds near the kinase domain. TAK1-TAB1-TAB2/TAB3
  complexes are recruited to and activated by K63-linked and unanchored polyubiquitin chains, which are bound by
  the TAB2/TAB3 zinc fingers, leading to autophosphorylation of the TAK1 activation loop (Thr184/Thr187/Ser192).
  Activated TAK1 phosphorylates MKK3/MKK6 and MKK4/MKK7, driving p38 and JNK MAP kinase cascades, and phosphorylates
  IKKbeta to activate canonical NF-kappaB signaling. TAK1 is the shared kinase relay downstream of TRAF6 and related
  ubiquitin ligases in IL-1 receptor, Toll-like receptor, TNF receptor, NOD-like receptor, antigen receptor and
  TGF-beta/BMP signaling, and it is inactivated by PP6/PP2A-type phosphatases. Other reported substrates include
  AMPK and STING. TAK1 acts mainly in the cytosol after recruitment to receptor-proximal complexes. Heterozygous
  MAP3K7 variants cause frontometaphyseal dysplasia 2 and cardiospondylocarpofacial syndrome.

    summary: TAK1 is a MAP3K, not a MAPK; the assay in this paper measured JNK1 activity downstream of TAK1.
    action: MODIFY
    reason: Full text of PMID:11865055 shows that kinase assays used ATF-2 or MBP as substrates of immunoprecipitated
      JNK1; TAK1 was manipulated with a catalytically inactive mutant and acts upstream of JNK1. TAK1 does not phosphorylate
      transcription-factor substrates as a MAP kinase. The essence (TAK1 kinase activity in the JNK pathway) is
      better captured by MAP kinase kinase kinase activity.
    proposed_replacement_terms:
    - id: GO:0004709
      label: MAP kinase kinase kinase activity
    supported_by:
    - reference_id: PMID:11865055
      supporting_text: After immunoprecipitation, an in vitro kinase assay was performed using ATF-2 or MBP as substrates.
    - reference_id: PMID:11865055
      supporting_text: The MAP3 kinase, TAK1, appears to be an essential component of this antiapoptotic pathway

    label: MAP kinase kinase kinase activity
  evidence_type: IDA
  original_reference_id: PMID:11460167
  qualifier: enables
  review:
    summary: 'Core activity: TAK1 phosphorylates and activates MKK3/MKK6 and MKK4/MKK7.'
    action: ACCEPT
    reason: Direct in vitro phosphorylation of MKK3/MKK6 and K63-ubiquitin-regulated phosphorylation of MKK6, plus

        directly regulated by K63-linked polyubiquitination

  - ensembl:ENSRNOP00000007654
  review:
    summary: Type II TGF-beta receptor binding transferred from rat; human data show TAK1 engagement via TbetaRI-TRAF6.
    action: MARK_AS_OVER_ANNOTATED
    reason: TGF-beta activates TAK1 through TbetaRI-bound TRAF6 and K63 ubiquitination of TAK1, not via a demonstrated
      direct TbetaRII-TAK1 binding function.
    supported_by:
    - reference_id: PMID:18758450
      supporting_text: Our data show that TGF-beta specifically activates TAK1 through interaction of TbetaRI with
        TRAF6, whereas activation of Smad2 is not dependent on TRAF6.
- term:

  original_reference_id: PMID:10094049
  qualifier: enables
  supporting_entities:
  - UniProtKB:Q9Y4K3
  review:
    summary: Interaction with the E3 ubiquitin ligase TRAF6.
    action: MODIFY
    reason: TRAF6 is a ubiquitin ligase whose association with TAK1 (directly or within the TAK1-TAB complex) promotes
      or regulates TAK1 activation by ubiquitin chains. Generic protein binding is uninformative; ubiquitin protein
      ligase binding captures the functional class of the interaction. This does not reject the reported interaction.
    proposed_replacement_terms:

      label: ubiquitin protein ligase binding
    supported_by:

      supporting_text: Here we show that the MAPKK kinase TAK1 acts upstream of NIK in the IL-1-activated signalling
        pathway and that TAK1 associates with TRAF6 during IL-1 signalling.

- term:
    id: GO:0008349
    label: MAP kinase kinase kinase kinase activity
  evidence_type: IEA
  original_reference_id: GO_REF:0000107
  qualifier: enables
  supporting_entities:
  - UniProtKB:Q62073
  - ensembl:ENSMUSP00000079734
  review:
    summary: MAP4K activity reflects TAK1 autophosphorylation events, not phosphorylation of a separate MAP3K.
    action: MODIFY
    reason: The Reactome reactions cited are "Auto phosphorylation of TAK1" / "Activation of recruited TAK1"; mapping
      a MAP3K autophosphorylating itself to MAP kinase kinase kinase kinase activity misrepresents its activity.
      The underlying Ser/Thr autophosphorylation is captured by protein serine/threonine kinase activity. The early
      report of TAK1 phosphorylating the MAP3K NIK (PMID:10094049) is not the basis of the modern model, where TAK1
      phosphorylates IKKbeta directly.
    proposed_replacement_terms:
    - id: GO:0004674
      label: protein serine/threonine kinase activity
    supported_by:
    - reference_id: PMID:10702308
      supporting_text: These results suggest that IL-1 and ectopic expression of TAB1 both activate TAK1 via autophosphorylation
        of Ser-192.
    - reference_id: PMID:10838074
      supporting_text: Autophosphorylation of two threonine residues in the activation loop of TAK1 was necessary
        for TAK1 activation.

    label: serine/threonine protein kinase complex
  evidence_type: IPI
  original_reference_id: PMID:22158122
  qualifier: part_of
  review:
    summary: 'ComplexPortal: TAK1 is part of a Ser/Thr protein kinase complex (TAK1-TAB).'
    action: ACCEPT
    reason: TAK1-TAB1-TAB2/TAB3 is a Ser/Thr kinase complex. ComplexPortal uses this generic term; the specific
      GO:0097076 TGF-beta activated kinase 1 complex is proposed as NEW (and is not currently an is_a child of GO:1902554
      in GO).
    supported_by:
    - reference_id: PMID:11460167
      supporting_text: Here we report the purification and identification of TRIKA2, which is composed of TAK1,
        TAB1 and TAB2, a protein kinase complex previously implicated in IKK activation through an unknown mechanism.

- term:
    id: GO:1990450
    label: linear polyubiquitin binding
  evidence_type: IDA
  original_reference_id: PMID:35952808
  qualifier: enables
  review:
    summary: Linear polyubiquitin binding from a TRIM56 study (abstract only).
    action: UNDECIDED
    reason: The abstract describes TRIM56 enhancing M1-linked chains conjugated to TAK1, i.e. covalent modification
      rather than demonstrated non-covalent linear-chain binding by TAK1. In the TAK1 complex, ubiquitin binding
      is performed by TAB2/TAB3 NZF domains. Without full text this cannot be adjudicated.

      supporting_text: TRIM56 enhanced the ubiquitination of TAK1, specifically enhanced the M1-linked polyubiquitin
        chains to TAK1, leading to the tight interactions of the TAK1-IKKα complex.
    - reference_id: PMID:15327770
      supporting_text: We also show that TAB2 binds to polyubiquitinated RIP following TNFalpha stimulation.

- term:
    id: GO:0097076
    label: transforming growth factor beta activated kinase 1 complex
  evidence_type: IDA
  original_reference_id: PMID:11460167
  qualifier: part_of
  review:
    summary: TAK1 is the catalytic subunit of the TAK1-TAB1-TAB2/TAB3 complex (TRIKA2).
    action: NEW
    reason: The purified IKK-activating factor TRIKA2 is composed of TAK1, TAB1 and TAB2; TAB3 substitutes for TAB2.
      GO:0097076 is defined as exactly this complex (catalytic TAK1 plus TAB regulatory subunits) but currently
      carries no annotations in GOA; ComplexPortal annotates TAK1 to the generic GO:1902554. This is a component
      membership, not a pathway-participation claim, so the NEW process bar does not apply; the PTHR46716 family
      review scopes GO:0097076 to SF1.
    supported_by:
    - reference_id: PMID:11460167
      supporting_text: Here we report the purification and identification of TRIKA2, which is composed of TAK1,
        TAB1 and TAB2, a protein kinase complex previously implicated in IKK activation through an unknown mechanism.
    - reference_id: PMID:8638164
      supporting_text: TAB1 and TAK1 were co-immunoprecipitated from mammalian cells.
    - reference_id: PMID:14633987
      supporting_text: These results suggest that TAB2 and TAB3 function redundantly as mediators of TAK1 activation
        in IL-1 and TNF signal transduction.

proposed_new_terms: []
suggested_questions:
- question: GO:0097076 (TGF-beta activated kinase 1 complex) has no annotations in GOA, and ComplexPortal annotates
    TAK1-TAB complexes to the generic GO:1902554 serine/threonine protein kinase complex. Should GO:0097076 be made
    is_a GO:1902554 (it is currently only under GO:1902911 protein kinase complex) and used for TAK1, TAB1, TAB2
    and TAB3?
- question: Is the 1999 TAK1-NIK-IKKalpha model (basis of the non-canonical NF-kappaB IMP annotation) still supported,
    or should TAK1 annotations to non-canonical NF-kappaB be retired in favour of direct IKKbeta activation?
- question: Which substrate-specific outputs of TAK1 (MKK3/6, MKK4/7, IKKbeta, AMPK, STING) are selected by TAB1
    versus TAB2/TAB3-containing complexes in a given receptor context?
suggested_experiments:
- description: Reconstitute TAK1-TAB1, TAK1-TAB2 and TAK1-TAB3 complexes with defined K63, M1 and hybrid unanchored
    chains and measure phosphorylation kinetics on MKK4, MKK6, MKK7 and IKKbeta.
  hypothesis: Ubiquitin chain architecture and TAB composition tune TAK1 substrate selection between MAPK and IKK
    branches.
- description: Compare kinase activity, autophosphorylation and substrate phosphorylation of FMD-associated MAP3K7
    variants (P485L, E70Q, V100E, G168R) with wild type in patient-derived fibroblasts.
  hypothesis: The recurrent P485L variant enhances TAB-dependent autoactivation without altering TAB2 binding, biasing
    downstream signaling.
